# मन के मोती — artwork prompts

These prompts define the shared visual identity for artwork generated for Pramila Singh's poetry book. The first five illustrations were generated with Gemini in October 2026.

## Base prompt

> You are creating a cohesive series of illustrations for "मन के मोती," a family memory book containing Hindi dohe written by Pramila Singh.
>
> Maintain this exact visual identity across every illustration:
>
> - Hand-painted Indian watercolor and gouache on warm, lightly textured handmade paper
> - Graceful traditional Indian visual language with a contemporary editorial-art sensibility
> - Warm burgundy, muted saffron, antique gold, earthy green, soft indigo and natural cream palette
> - Gentle, dignified, reflective and emotionally warm
> - Symbolic visual storytelling rather than overly literal scenes
> - Delicate brushwork, subtle paper grain, restrained details and soft natural lighting
> - Portrait orientation, using Gemini's native 3:4 aspect ratio
> - High resolution, preferably at least 1600 x 2000 pixels when supported
> - Keep every essential subject fully inside an 8% safe margin on all sides; decorative paper texture and nonessential washes may extend to the edges
> - Use a true full-bleed painted composition extending continuously to all four canvas edges
> - No white or cream blank outer border, blank paper margin, paper mat, decorative frame, inset illustration panel or page margin
> - Extend sky, landscape, foliage, watercolor washes and painted paper texture all the way to every edge
> - Preserve the downloaded image's original dimensions and bytes; do not crop, stretch, resize-to-fill or otherwise alter the generated composition
> - Place the strongest visual elements around the edges and lower portions while keeping some calm, lower-detail space through the center for possible text overlay on phones
> - No written words, letters, captions, calligraphy, signatures, numbers, logos or decorative typography
> - Do not imitate any named artist
> - Avoid glossy 3D rendering, photographic realism, cartoon styling and oversaturated colors
> - Religious figures and symbols must be portrayed respectfully
> - People should have natural anatomy, hands and expressions
> - The completed image should feel like one page from a treasured, hand-illustrated Indian poetry book
>
> For each request, first understand the meaning and emotional contrast of the supplied doha, then express that meaning as a single unified scene.
>
> Generate exactly one finished illustration without adding any text.

For new downloads, prefer a true full-bleed Gemini result. If Gemini still bakes in a genuine near-uniform blank paper or mat border, preserve the untouched download in session scratch and trim only the verified blank-margin pixels before importing the final `art/` file. Never apply a fixed crop or remove painted lines, intentional washes, objects or edge details; leave ambiguous edges unchanged.

## व्योम

- Note ID: `notes_20240423113656`
- Image: `notes_20240423113656.jpg`

> नीले नीले व्योम पे, ढूंढे सभी मयंक।
> अपने अपने चांद को, झट से भर लूं अंक।

> Create the next illustration in the established "मन के मोती" style.
>
> The doha imagines people searching the vast blue sky for the moon, while each heart longs to embrace its own beloved "चाँद." Interpret the moon both literally and as a symbol of someone precious.
>
> Show an expansive indigo-blue evening sky with one luminous full moon. Around the lower edges, place a few graceful silhouettes looking upward from separate Indian courtyards and terraces. In the foreground, show a motherly figure gently gathering a pool of moonlight toward her heart as though embracing someone beloved. Small reflections of the moon may appear in brass bowls or quiet water, suggesting that everyone finds their own moon.
>
> Keep the center spacious and luminous. The mood should convey wonder, affection and intimate longing rather than sadness.
>
> No text, lettering or multiple literal moons.

## शशांक

- Note ID: `notes_20240423113758`
- Image: `notes_20240423113758.jpg`

> शिव भक्ति से रोहिणी,राखे प्रिय के प्रान।
> शिव मस्तक पर बैठके,हुए शशांक महान‌।

> Create the next illustration in the established "मन के मोती" style.
>
> This devotional doha refers to Rohini's devotion to Shiva and to Shashank—the moon—receiving an honored place upon Shiva's head.
>
> Show Lord Shiva seated in serene meditation beneath a night sky, portrayed respectfully in a traditional poetic style. A luminous crescent moon rests upon his matted hair, spreading cool silver-blue light. Near the lower edge, show Rohini in devoted prayer, surrounded by a restrained glow that connects her folded hands to the crescent.
>
> Include subtle Himalayan forms, a quiet stream suggestive of the Ganga, rudraksha and a distant temple bell. Keep the scene calm and sacred, emphasizing devotion, protection and the moon's transformation from vulnerability into divine honor.
>
> No text, inscriptions or dramatic battle imagery.

> The doha offers reassurance that in moments of suffering, a sincere and compassionate prayer is heard by the divine.
>
> Show a solitary person seated in prayer beside a small diya during a difficult night. The outer edges may contain rain, dark clouds, a rough path and bent branches, symbolizing hardship. Around the praying figure, let the diya create a warm protective circle of saffron and antique-gold light.
>
> A gentle beam of light should descend through an opening in the clouds, suggesting that the prayer has been heard without depicting a specific deity. Include a few new leaves or a distant clearing along the path as quiet signs of hope.
>
> The mood should move naturally from pain and vulnerability toward comfort, faith and reassurance. Avoid making the suffering frightening or melodramatic.
>
> No text or lettering.

>
> No labels, written mantras or inscriptions.

> This socially conscious doha warns that damaging the environment makes human beings ill, while nature itself pleads with us to consume resources responsibly.
>
> Create one unified landscape transitioning from environmental harm toward restoration. Along the darker outer edge, show a weakened tree, a shrinking stream, smoke in the distance and a few subdued human silhouettes affected by unhealthy air. Nature should appear wounded but not grotesque.
>
> Toward the warmer side, show human hands carefully planting a young sapling, conserving water and allowing green leaves to return. A river or vine may flow through the composition, changing gradually from muted grey-blue to healthy green and gold. Include birds returning near the restored area.
>
> The emotional center should be nature's quiet plea for balance, restraint and responsible consumption. Avoid a simplistic split-screen, corporate imagery, protest signs or apocalyptic destruction.
>
> No text, logos or lettering.

## शुभ

- Note ID: `notes_20240423114030`
- Image: `notes_20240423114030.jpg`

> बिल्ली काटे राह जो, मत करना प्रस्थान।
> जल से भरा कलश मिले, शुभ लक्षण यह जान‌

> Create the next illustration in the established "मन के मोती" style.
>
> The doha reflects traditional Indian ideas of शुभ and अशुभ signs: a cat crossing one's path is treated as a warning not to begin a journey, while encountering a water-filled kalash is regarded as an auspicious sign.
>
> Compose a poetic village or old-town pathway at dawn. On one side, a dark cat quietly crosses a receding path beneath a soft area of shadow. On the other side, a luminous brass kalash filled with clear water rests near a welcoming doorway, decorated subtly with mango leaves and a small marigold accent. Warm morning light touches the kalash while the cat remains in cooler muted tones.
>
> Present the two omens as parts of one balanced symbolic scene, not as a split-screen diagram. The emotional movement should travel from hesitation and uncertainty toward warmth and auspicious hope.
>
> No text or lettering.

Several flattering companions lean close with theatrical expressions, but their masks or reflections subtly reveal insincerity.
>
> At the calmer side, show a thoughtful person stepping back into clear warm light, maintaining a respectful boundary marked by a simple circle of flower petals or a low garden edge. Keep the satire humane rather than frightening or accusatory. Use burgundy and gold for surface sweetness, indigo shadow for hidden self-interest.
>
> No identifiable people, written money, labels, text, words, letters, numbers, signatures or logos.

> This devotional kundaliya draws on the teaching associated with Sant Ravidas that sincere faith and a pure heart can make sacred presence available even in an ordinary water basin. Portray humility, devotion and inner cleansing without written quotations or claims of guaranteed miracles.
>
> Show Sant Ravidas respectfully in a modest workshop courtyard at dawn, wearing simple clothing and pausing from daily craft work beside a plain, clean metal water basin. In the basin's reflection, suggest the Ganga through luminous flowing water, lotus petals and a distant mountain-river vision, while the actual courtyard remains humble and grounded. His folded hands and serene expression convey heartfelt devotion.
>
> Nearby, a few diverse listeners sit at the same level, sharing quiet prayer. Let dark tangled threads at one edge become clear flowing strands and fresh leaves near the basin, symbolizing release from wrongdoing and sorrow. Use a diya, simple tools and an open sky, but no books, scrolls, written prayer or labels.
>
> Use natural cream, river blue, earthy brown, muted saffron, leaf green and antique gold. No caste hierarchy imagery, miracle spectacle, inscriptions, mantras, text, words, letters, numbers, signatures or logos.

> लड्डू इसके खाइए, चाटेंगे फिर हाथ।
> 3.
> याददाश्त बढ़िया रहे, खाते जो बादाम।
> मेवा यह स्वादिष्ट है, है प्रोटीन तमाम।
> 4.
> सूखें जब अंगूर तो, किशमिश पाती रूप।
> मेवा मीठी यह बने, अद्भुत और अनूप।
> 5.
> चिलगोजा मेवा सुनो, गुणकारी भरपूर।
> स्वाद विटामिन के लिए, काफी है मशहूर।
> 6.
> मेवा इक मशहूर है, पिस्ता उसका नाम।
> बर्फी इसकी खाइए, भूलें स्वाद तमाम।
> 7.
> मीठे में मेवा लगे, स्वर्ण जड़ित ज्यों रत्न।
> शोभा इनकी गजब हो, मिलते जब बहु यत्न।

This dry fruit is delicious and contains abundant protein. 4. When grapes dry, they take the form of raisins. They become a sweet dry fruit, wonderful and distinctive. 5. Listen: the chilgoza pine nut is full of beneficial qualities. It is well known for its taste and vitamins. 6. There is a famous dry fruit called the pistachio. Eat its barfi, and every other flavour will be forgotten. 7. Dry fruits in sweets look like gems set in gold. Their beauty is amazing when they are obtained with much effort.
